# Adafruit and SparkFun PCB project work queue

Generated from the Adafruit and SparkFun GitHub repository trees. The queue is ordered from most recently created repository to oldest. Each row is one repository containing at least one `.brd` or `.kicad_pcb` source file.

Future agents should work from the top down: identify the main source board, skip production panels when a source board exists, declare the project in the appropriate `project_data/*/working.yaml`, then add missing purchased components through the populate flow. Rows marked **ADDED** already have a project declaration; **TODO** still need declaration and component review.

Inventory: 1466 qualifying repositories.
